- **pmtui-message**: Talk to another session's agent. Ask a question, give a finding, or answer a question you were asked. Invoke when another session owns something you need. ([hung-phan/pmtui](https://github.com/hung-phan/pmtui/tree/HEAD/skills/pmtui-message) · ★6)
- **pmtui-spawn**: Dispatch one independent part of your task to a child job. A child job is one headless agent run that does the task, reports a result and exits. Invoke when another agent could do that part without you. Dispatch it with one complete Message, poll for its result, and act on the JSON. Use --session instead when the part needs an agent that stays, which makes a persistent session you can message. ([hung-phan/pmtui](https://github.com/hung-phan/pmtui/tree/HEAD/skills/pmtui-spawn) · ★6)
- **pmtui-ui-testing**: How to render and drive the pmtui dashboard under tmux, to test the UI end-to-end. Launch it headless on a scratch tmux server and a scratch registry. Navigate with send-keys, and read the screen back with capture-pane. Set up a render state by seeding .project-state/ files. Invoke whenever you change what the dashboard draws. That includes src/bin/pmtui/, the render code for a row, preview, help or chrome, the glyphs and the keybar. Invoke it also when you want to SEE a result instead of trusting a unit test.
